	#Politics of Euroscepticism
	focus = {
		id = HUN_politics_of_euroscepticism
		icon = NOR_eu_distance
		x = -2
		y = 2
		relative_position_id = HUN_foreign_policy_of_hungary

		prerequisite = { focus = HUN_EU_membership_question }

		cost = 5

		available = {
			NOT = { has_government = democratic }
		}

		search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_ARMY }

		completion_reward = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus HUN_politics_of_euroscepticism"
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = neutrality
				popularity = 0.05
			}
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = communism
				popularity = 0.05
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_idea = EU_member }
				set_temp_variable = { modify_eurosceptic = 0.09 }
				EU_eurosceptic_change = yes
				leaving_EU = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = EU_candidate }
				custom_effect_tooltip = POL_lpr_not_eu_tt
				clr_country_flag = EU_candidate
				set_temp_variable = { modify_eurosceptic = 0.07 }
				EU_eurosceptic_change = yes
			}
			else = {
				set_temp_variable = { modify_eurosceptic = 0.05 }
				EU_eurosceptic_change = yes
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = TT_ALL_EU_MEMBER_NATIONS_GAIN

		}

					}

					limit = { has_idea = small_medium_business_owners }
					swap_ideas = {

						add_idea = labour_unions
					}
				}

					limit = { has_idea = industrial_conglomerates }

						add_idea = labour_unions
					}
				}

					limit = { has_idea = landowners }
					swap_ideas = {
						remove_idea = landowners
						add_idea = labour_unions
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { has_idea = farmers }
						swap_ideas = {
							remove_idea = farmers
							add_idea = labour_unions
						}
					}
				else_if = {

						add_idea = labour_unions
					}

						add_idea = labour_unions

						add_idea = labour_unions
					}
				}

		}

			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus HUN_magyar_munkaspart"
			add_political_power = 125
			set_temp_variable = { party_index = 4 }
			set_temp_variable = { party_popularity_increase = 0.10 }
			set_temp_variable = { temp_outlook_increase = 0.10 }
			add_relative_party_popularity = yes
			hidden_effect = {
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { ruling_party = 4 } } }
					set_temp_variable = { rul_party_temp = 4 }
					set_temp_variable = { enable_elections = 0 }
					change_ruling_party_effect = yes
				}
			}
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 100
		}

	#Withdrawal from Western organizations
	focus = {
		id = HUN_withdrawal_from_western_organizations
		icon = POL_dont_need_eu
		x = 0
		y = 1
		relative_position_id = HUN_magyar_munkaspart

		cost = 6

		search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_POLITICAL }
		prerequisite = { focus = HUN_magyar_munkaspart }

		available = {
			emerging_communist_state_are_in_power = yes
		}

		completion_reward = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus HUN_withdrawal_from_western_organizations"
			add_political_power = 50
			set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 5 }
			change_domestic_influence_percentage = yes
			if = {
				limit = { has_idea = EU_member }
				leaving_EU = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_idea = NATO_member }
				NATO_leave = yes
			}
